* SOC BY SHIVANI/ARCHNA FOR CHARM: 4000004392 ON 28/12/2021 FOR BATCH BLOCKING
DATA : lt_batch_block  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF yrva_batch_block,
       lwa_batch_block LIKE LINE OF lt_batch_block,
       lv_qty_avl      TYPE kwmeng,
       lt_mard         TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF mard,
       lwa_mard        LIKE LINE OF lt_mard.

** -> Begin of changes by of Sarfraz Ali on 11.11.2024 12:04:47 for ATC

* ONLY RUN FOR YRVR012 TRANSACTION
IF sy-tcode = 'YRVR012'.

* SELECT ALL THE ENTRIES TO GET THE SPECIAL BATCHES AND THEIR BLOCK STATUS
  SELECT * FROM yrva_batch_block
    INTO TABLE lt_batch_block.
  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
    CLEAR: lt_batch_block.
  ELSE.
    SORT lt_batch_block BY batch.
  ENDIF.

* SELECT THE TOTAL QUANTITY FROM MARD TABLE
  READ TABLE tekpo INDEX 1.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    SELECT * FROM mard
       INTO TABLE lt_mard
       WHERE matnr = tekpo-matnr
       AND   werks = i_ekko-reswk
       AND   lgort = 'WH01'.
    IF sy-subrc = 0.
      SORT lt_mard BY matnr werks lgort.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.

* POPULATE THE TOTAL QUANTITY IN YRVA_BATCH_BLOCK, IF BLANK
  LOOP AT lt_batch_block INTO lwa_batch_block
    WHERE material = tekpo-matnr.
    IF lwa_batch_block-total_qty IS INITIAL.
      READ TABLE lt_mard INTO lwa_mard
        WITH KEY matnr = tekpo-matnr
                 werks = i_ekko-reswk
                 lgort = 'WH01' BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        lwa_batch_block-total_qty = lwa_mard-labst.
        MODIFY yrva_batch_block FROM lwa_batch_block.
        MODIFY lt_batch_block FROM lwa_batch_block TRANSPORTING total_qty.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDLOOP.

* THIS CHECK WILL ONLY RUN IF THERE IS A BATCH MAINTAINED FOR THE SALES ORDER
  READ TABLE tekpo INDEX 1.
  IF sy-subrc = 0.
    IF  tekpo-matnr IS NOT INITIAL
    AND tekpo-charg IS NOT INITIAL.       "FOR ORDERS WITH A BATCH
      READ TABLE lt_batch_block INTO lwa_batch_block
        WITH KEY material = tekpo-matnr
                 batch    = tekpo-charg BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        lv_qty_avl = lwa_batch_block-block_qty - tekpo-menge.
        IF lwa_batch_block-block_status = lc_x.
          MESSAGE 'CAN NOT CREATE ORDER AS THIS BATCH QUANTITY IS BLOCKED' TYPE 'E'.
        ELSEIF lwa_batch_block-block_status <> lc_x.
          lwa_batch_block-total_qty = lwa_batch_block-total_qty - tekpo-menge.
          lwa_batch_block-block_qty = lwa_batch_block-block_qty - tekpo-menge.
          MODIFY yrva_batch_block FROM lwa_batch_block.
        ENDIF.
        CLEAR: lwa_batch_block.
      ENDIF.
    ELSEIF tekpo-matnr IS NOT INITIAL
      AND  tekpo-charg IS INITIAL.        "FOR ORDERS WITHOUT A BATCH
      SORT lt_batch_block BY material.
      READ TABLE lt_batch_block INTO lwa_batch_block
        WITH KEY material = tekpo-matnr BINARY SEARCH.
      IF sy-subrc = 0.
        lv_qty_avl = lwa_batch_block-total_qty.
        LOOP AT lt_batch_block INTO lwa_batch_block
          WHERE material = tekpo-matnr.
          lv_qty_avl = lv_qty_avl - lwa_batch_block-block_qty.
        ENDLOOP.

        lv_qty_avl = lv_qty_avl - tekpo-menge.
** SOC BY CHILUKURI TRIPURA REDDY/ARCHNA/VISHAL CHARM : 4000007331
        IF lv_qty_avl < 0.
          LOOP AT lt_batch_block INTO lwa_batch_block
                                 WHERE material = tekpo-matnr.
            lwa_batch_block-total_qty = 0.
            MODIFY yrva_batch_block FROM lwa_batch_block.
            MODIFY lt_batch_block FROM lwa_batch_block TRANSPORTING total_qty.
            CLEAR : lwa_batch_block.
          ENDLOOP.
          LOOP AT lt_batch_block INTO lwa_batch_block
           WHERE material = tekpo-matnr.
            IF lwa_batch_block-total_qty IS INITIAL.
              READ TABLE lt_mard INTO lwa_mard
                WITH KEY matnr = tekpo-matnr
                         werks = i_ekko-reswk
                         lgort = 'WH01' BINARY SEARCH.
              IF sy-subrc = 0.
                lwa_batch_block-total_qty = lwa_mard-labst.
                MODIFY yrva_batch_block FROM lwa_batch_block.
                MODIFY lt_batch_block FROM lwa_batch_block TRANSPORTING total_qty.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
          ENDLOOP.
          IF   tekpo-matnr IS NOT INITIAL
          AND  tekpo-charg IS INITIAL.        "FOR ORDERS WITHOUT A BATCH
            SORT lt_batch_block BY material.
            READ TABLE lt_batch_block INTO lwa_batch_block
              WITH KEY material = tekpo-matnr BINARY SEARCH.
            IF sy-subrc = 0.
              lv_qty_avl = lwa_batch_block-total_qty.
              LOOP AT lt_batch_block INTO lwa_batch_block
                WHERE material = tekpo-matnr.
                lv_qty_avl = lv_qty_avl - lwa_batch_block-block_qty.
              ENDLOOP.
            ENDIF.
          ENDIF.
          lv_qty_avl = lv_qty_avl - tekpo-menge.
        ENDIF.
** EOC BY CHILUKURI TRIPURA REDDY/ARCHNA/VISHAL CHARM : 4000007331
        IF lv_qty_avl < 0.
          MESSAGE 'CAN NOT CREATE ORDER AS THIS BATCH QUANTITY IS BLOCKED' TYPE 'E'.
        ELSEIF lv_qty_avl >= 0.
          LOOP AT lt_batch_block INTO lwa_batch_block
            WHERE material = tekpo-matnr.
            lwa_batch_block-total_qty = lwa_batch_block-total_qty - tekpo-menge.
            MODIFY yrva_batch_block FROM lwa_batch_block.
          ENDLOOP.
        ENDIF.
      ENDIF.
    ENDIF.
  ENDIF.
ENDIF.

                     ebeln  TYPE ebeln, "PURCHASING DOCUMENT NUMBER
                     ebelp  TYPE ebelp, "ITEM NUMBER OF PURCHASING DOCUMENT

                     netwr  TYPE bwert, "NET ORDER VALUE IN PO CURRENCY
                     effwr  TYPE effwr,
                     packno TYPE packno,
                     pstyp  TYPE pstyp,
                   END OF ty_ekpo.

            TYPES : BEGIN OF ty_esuh,
                      packno   TYPE packno,
                      sumlimit TYPE sumlimit,
                    END OF ty_esuh.



            DATA: my_esuh TYPE LINE OF mmsrv_esuh.
            DATA : ex_esuc TYPE TABLE OF esuc.
            DATA : wa_esuc TYPE esuc.


            DATA: i_poitm  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_ekpo,
                  wa_poitm TYPE ty_ekpo,
                  i_esuh   TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ty_esuh,
                  wa_esuh  TYPE ty_esuh,
                  i_itm    TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF ekpo,
                  wa_ekpo  TYPE bekpo.

            DATA: amt     TYPE bwert,
                  amt_ext TYPE bwert,
                  per_amt TYPE bwert,
                  new_amt TYPE bwert.

            SELECT ebeln
                   ebelp
                   loekz
                   netwr
                   packno
                   pstyp
              FROM ekpo INTO CORRESPONDING FIELDS OF TABLE i_poitm
                WHERE ebeln = i_ekko-ebeln.

            SELECT
                packno
                sumlimit
                FROM esuh INTO TABLE i_esuh
                FOR ALL ENTRIES IN i_poitm
                WHERE packno = i_poitm-packno.

            LOOP AT i_poitm INTO wa_poitm.
              IF wa_poitm-loekz NE 'L' AND wa_poitm-loekz NE 'S'.
                amt_ext = wa_poitm-netwr + amt_ext.
              ENDIF.

              IF wa_poitm-pstyp = 9.
                READ TABLE i_esuh INTO wa_esuh WITH KEY packno = wa_poitm-packno.
                IF sy-subrc = 0 .
                  amt_ext = wa_esuh-sumlimit + amt_ext.

                ENDIF.


              ENDIF.


            ENDLOOP.


*****
*  MOVE-CORRESPONDING I_EKPO TO I_ITM.
*APPEND I_EKPO TO I_ITM.
*
            DATA: lv_esuc_chk TYPE ebeln.
            LOOP AT tekpo INTO wa_ekpo.
              IF wa_ekpo-loekz NE 'L' AND wa_ekpo-loekz NE 'S'.
                amt = amt + wa_ekpo-netwr.
              ENDIF.
              IF wa_ekpo-pstyp = 9 .
                CALL FUNCTION 'MS_READ_LIMITS'
                  EXPORTING
                    packno          = wa_ekpo-packno
                  IMPORTING
                    e_esuh          = my_esuh
                  TABLES
                    limit_tab       = ex_esuc
                  EXCEPTIONS
                    no_limits_found = 1
                    OTHERS          = 2.
                IF sy-subrc <> 0.
* MESSAGE ID SY-MSGID TYPE SY-MSGTY NUMBER SY-MSGNO
*         WITH SY-MSGV1 SY-MSGV2 SY-MSGV3 SY-MSGV4.
                ELSE.
                  READ TABLE ex_esuc INTO wa_esuc INDEX 1.
                  IF sy-subrc = 0 .
                    amt = amt + wa_esuc-limit.
                    lv_esuc_chk = wa_esuc-ebeln.
                  ENDIF.


                ENDIF.




              ENDIF.



            ENDLOOP.

*******IF NEW AMOUNT(NETWR) DURING EDITING IS GREATER THAN 25%
*******OF PREVIOUS VALUE THEN SYSTEM SHOULD GIVE WARNING MESSAGE.
*BREAK-POINT.
*SOC RANVIJAY/MANAS SIR DT 17.03.2023 CHARM NO.- 4000006085
            DATA: l_it1(30) TYPE c.
            IF i_ekko-bsart = 'ZS' OR
                         i_ekko-bsart = 'ZQ' OR i_ekko-bsart = 'ZC' OR
                            i_ekko-bsart = 'ZCON'.
              IF lv_esuc_chk IS INITIAL.
                per_amt = amt_ext / 100 * 25.
                new_amt = amt_ext + per_amt.
                IF amt GT new_amt AND new_amt IS NOT INITIAL.
*    MESSAGE 'THE COST OF PO HAS BEEN RAISED BY MORE THAN 25%' TYPE 'W'.
                  CONCATENATE 'for item no.' tekpo-ebelp INTO l_it1 SEPARATED BY space.
                  mmpur_message 'W' 'YM01' '000' 'PO value increased by more than 25%.'(433) tekpo-ebeln l_it1 'Pls rechk or tick to proceed.'.
                ENDIF.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
*EOC RANVIJAY/MANAS SIR DT 17.03.2023 CHARM NO.- 4000006085


            DATA: l_it(30) TYPE c.
            IF i_ekko-bsart = 'ZF' OR i_ekko-bsart = 'ZP' OR
                         i_ekko-bsart = 'ZD' OR i_ekko-bsart = 'ZC' OR
                             i_ekko-bsart = 'ZMRP' OR i_ekko-bsart = 'ZONM' OR
                      i_ekko-bsart = 'ZPGG' OR i_ekko-bsart = 'ZPRJ'.


              per_amt = amt_ext / 100 * 25.
              new_amt = amt_ext + per_amt.
              IF amt GT new_amt AND new_amt IS NOT INITIAL.
*    MESSAGE 'THE COST OF PO HAS BEEN RAISED BY MORE THAN 25%' TYPE 'W'.
                CONCATENATE 'for item no.' tekpo-ebelp INTO l_it SEPARATED BY space.
                mmpur_message 'W' 'YM01' '000' 'PO value increased by more than 25%.'(433) tekpo-ebeln l_it 'Pls rechk or tick to proceed.'.
              ENDIF.
            ENDIF.
